Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The electrochemistry operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 770. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 484 with 4 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to electrochemistry theory, we decompose the problem into 770 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 484. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(4/n). QED.
